You wake up with dirt in your mouth and blood on your hands that isn't yours. The sky is the wrong color — not sunset-wrong, wound-wrong. Gina Porter. Carpenter. From Columbus, Ohio. That was yesterday. This is now. You have: Dead Phone, Unnamed photograph, Wedding ring, Oath Ring, Carpenter's square. That's everything you own in this world. Haven. Twelve people. The fire is lit.
